only sources, scripts, instances and documentation.')\n   201\t\n   202\t\n   203\tif __name__ == '__main__':\n   204\t    main()\n\n     1\t//----------------------------------------------------------------------\n     2\t// Solver routine for the Bin Packing Problem with Conflict Classes.\n     3\t//\n     4\t// Preserves the supplied randomized First Fit heuristic for the initial\n     5\t// incumbent, then calls the exact branch-and-price implemented separately.\n     6\t//----------------------------------------------------------------------\n     7\t#include \"binpacksolver.h\"\n     8\t#include \"branchprice.h\"\n     9\t#include <algorithm>\n    10\t#include <chrono>\n    11\t#include <iostream>\n    12\t#include <numeric>\n    13\t#include <random>\n    14\t#include <vector>\n    15\t\n    16\tusing namespace std;\n    17\t\n    18\tstatic mt19937_64 rng(1); // seeded in SolveBinPacking from data.seed\n    19\t\n    20\tstatic double RandomUniform01()\n    21\t{\n    22\t\treturn uniform_real_distribution<double>(0.0, 1.0)(rng);\n    23\t}\n    24\t\n    25\t// Priority of an item in a round of the heuristic; items are packed in\n    26\t// decreasing order of priority.\n    27\t//   size    : size of the item divided by C, in (0,1];\n    28\t//   nclasses: number of classes that contain the item divided by the maximum,\n    29\t//             over all items, of that number, in [0,1];\n    30\t//   bias    : \"tendency\" number in [0,1].\n    31\t// For bias = 0 the priority is the size, so the order is First Fit Decreasing.\n    32\t// For bias = 1 the priority is nclasses, so the items that take part in more\n    33\t// conflict classes come first. For bias = 1/2 the priority is a random number\n    34\t// that depends neither on the size nor on the number of classes. In between,\n    35\t// the priority is a convex combination of the random number and the size (for\n    36\t// bias < 1/2) or the random number and nclasses (for bias > 1/2).\n    37\tstatic double ItemPriority(double size, double nclasses, double bias)\n    38\t{\n    39\t\tdouble r = RandomUniform01();\n    40\t\tif (bias <= 0.5)\n    41\t\t{\n    42\t\t\tdouble lambda = 1.0 - 2.0 * bias; // 1 at bias=0, 0 at bias=1/2\n    43\t\t\treturn lambda * size + (1.0 - lambda) * r;\n    44\t\t}\n    45\t\telse\n    46\t\t{\n    47\t\t\tdouble mu = 2.0 * bias - 1.0; // 0 at bias=1/2, 1 at bias=1\n    48\t\t\treturn mu * nclasses + (1.0 - mu) * r;\n    49\t\t}\n    50\t}\n    51\t\n    52\t// One round: sorts the items by ItemPriority (ties: larger size first) and\n    53\t// packs them in First Fit style, never putting two conflicting items in the same\n    54\t// bin. A new bin is opened only if no open bin accepts the item. Always\n    55\t// produces a feasible packing, since each item has size <= C.\n    56\tstatic bool FirstFitRound(const BinPackingData &data, double bias, vector<vector<int>> &bins,\n    57\t                          chrono::steady_clock::time_point deadline)\n    58\t{\n    59\t\tint n = data.n;\n    60\t\tint maxcls = 0;\n    61\t\tfor (int i = 1; i <= n; i++)\n    62\t\t\tmaxcls = max(maxcls, (int)data.classes_of[i].size());\n    63\t\n    64\t\tvector<double> prio(n + 1, 0.0);\n    65\t\tvector<int> order;\n    66\t\tfor (int i = 1; i <= n; i++)\n    67\t\t{\n    68\t\t\tdouble s = (double)data.size[i] / (double)data.C;\n    69\t\t\tdouble c = maxcls > 0 ? (double)data.classes_of[i].size() / maxcls : 0.0;\n    70\t\t\tprio[i] = ItemPriority(s, c, bias);\n    71\t\t\torder.push_back(i);\n    72\t\t}\n    73\t\tsort(order.begin(), order.end(),\n    74\t\t     [&](int a, int b)\n    75\t\t     {\n    76\t\t\t     if (prio[a] != prio[b])\n    77\t\t\t\t     return prio[a] > prio[b];\n    78\t\t\t     if (data.size[a] != data.size[b])\n    79\t\t\t\t     return data.size[a] > data.size[b];\n    80\t\t\t     return a < b;\n    81\t\t     });\n    82\t\n    83\t\tbins.clear();\n    84\t\tvector<long long> load;\n    85\t\tvector<vector<char>> used; // used[b][t] = 1 if bin b has an item of class t\n    86\t\tfor (size_t p = 0; p < order.size(); p++)\n    87\t\t{\n    88\t\t\tif (chrono::steady_clock::now() >= deadline)\n    89\t\t\t\treturn false;\n    90\t\t\tint i = order[p];\n    91\t\t\tint chosen = -1;\n    92\t\t\tfor (size_t b = 0; b < bins.size() && chosen < 0; b++)\n    93\t\t\t{\n    94\t\t\t\tif (data.size[i] > data.C - load[b])\n    95\t\t\t\t\tcontinue;\n    96\t\t\t\tbool ok = true;\n    97\t\t\t\tfor (size_t a = 0; a < data.classes_of[i].size() && ok; a++)\n    98\t\t\t\t\tif (used[b][data.classes_of[i][a]])\n    99\t\t\t\t\t\tok = false;\n   100\t\t\t\tif (ok)\n   101\t\t\t\t\tchosen = (int)b;\n   102\t\t\t}\n   103\t\t\tif (chosen < 0)\n   104\t\t\t{\n   105\t\t\t\tchosen = (int)bins.size();\n   106\t\t\t\tbins.push_back(vector<int>());\n   107\t\t\t\tload.push_back(0);\n   108\t\t\t\tused.push_back(vector<char>(data.k, 0));\n   109\t\t\t}\n   110\t\t\tbins[chosen].push_back(i);\n   111\t\t\tload[chosen] += data.size[i];\n   112\t\t\tfor (size_t a = 0; a < data.classes_of[i].size(); a++)\n   113\t\t\t\tused[chosen][data.classes_of[i][a]] = 1;\n   114\t\t}\n   115\t\treturn true;\n   116\t}\n   117\t\n   118\t// Randomized First Fit Decreasing with conflicts. In each of at most\n   119\t// `max_rounds` rounds, a bias in [0,1] is drawn uniformly at random and used to\n   120\t// order all the items of that round. The best packing found is kept in data.\n   121\tstatic void RandomizedFFD(BinPackingData &data, int max_rounds, double time_limit,\n   122\t                          const chrono::steady_clock::time_point &start)\n   123\t{\n   124\t\tvector<vector<int>> bins;\n   125\t\tfor (int round = 0; round < max_rounds; round++)\n   126\t\t{\n   127\t\t\tdouble elapsed = chrono::duration<double>(chrono::steady_clock::now() - start).count();\n   128\t\t\tif (elapsed >= time_limit)\n   129\t\t\t\tbreak; // respect the initialization budget\n   130\t\t\tif (data.best_value <= data.lower_bound)\n   131\t\t\t\tbreak; // optimal\n   132\t\t\tdouble bias = RandomUniform01();\n   133\t\t\tif (!FirstFitRound(data, bias, bins,\n   134\t\t\t                   start + chrono::duration_cast<chrono::steady_clock::duration>(\n   135\t\t\t                               chrono::duration<double>(time_limit))))\n   136\t\t\t\tbreak;\n   137\t\t\tif (data.UpdateBest(bins))\n   138\t\t\t\tcout << \"[Heuristic: randomized FFD] round \" << round + 1 << \" (bias \" << bias\n   139\t\t\t\t     << \"): \" << data.best_value << \" bins\" << endl;\n   140\t\t}\n   141\t}\n   142\t\n   143\tvoid SolveBinPacking(BinPackingData &data, double time_limit)\n   144\t{\n   145\t\tconst auto start = chrono::steady_clock::now();\n   146\t\tconst auto deadline = start + chrono::duration_cast<chrono::steady_clock::duration>(\n   147\t\t                                  chrono::duration<double>(time_limit));\n   148\t\trng.seed(data.seed);\n   149\t\t// A fallback exists even when T=0. The supplied randomized FFD remains the\n   150\t\t// source of the initial incumbent and its bins seed the restricted master.\n   151\t\tvector<vector<int>> fallback;\n   152\t\tfor (int i = 1; i <= data.n; ++i)\n   153\t\t\tfallback.push_back({i});\n   154\t\tdata.UpdateBest(fallback);\n   155\t\tdata.lower_bound = max(data.lower_bound, data.TrivialLowerBound());\n   156\t\tRandomizedFFD(data, 1000, min(0.25, time_limit * 0.05), start);\n   157\t\tif (data.best_value > data.lower_bound && chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline)\n   158\t\t\tBranchAndPrice(data, deadline);\n   159\t\n   160\t\tdata.elapsed = chrono::duration<double>(chrono::steady_clock::now() - start).count();\n   161\t}\n     1\t//----------------------------------------------------------------------\n     2\t// Bin Packing Problem with Conflict Classes.\n     3\t//\n     4\t// This header defines BinPackingData, the type that encapsulates the whole\n     5\t// input of an instance, together with the value and the best solution found\n     6\t// so far (in the same spirit as TSP_Data in ex_tsp.cpp). A pointer/reference\n     7\t// to this object is all that the solver routine needs.\n     8\t//\n     9\t// Conventions: the items are I = {1,...,n} (vectors indexed by items have\n    10\t// n+1 positions; position 0 is not used), and the conflict classes are\n    11\t// S_0,...,S_{k-1} (index t in 0..k-1). Two items i != j cannot be packed in\n    12\t// the same bin if there is a class t with i,j in S_t.\n    13\t//----------------------------------------------------------------------\n    14\t#ifndef BINPACKING_H\n    15\t#define BINPACKING_H\n    16\t\n    17\t#include <algorithm>\n    18\t#include <iostream>\n    19\t#include <sstream>\n    20\t#include <string>\n    21\t#include <vector>\n    22\t\n    23\tclass BinPackingData\n    24\t{\n    25\t  public:\n    26\t\t// ---------------------------- input data -----------------------…1328 tokens truncated…9\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n   170\t\t\t}\n   171\t\t\tfor (int i = 1; i <= n; i++)\n   172\t\t\t\tif (seen[i] != 1)\n   173\t\t\t\t{\n   174\t\t\t\t\tstd::ostringstream o;\n   175\t\t\t\t\to << \"item \" << i << \" is not packed\";\n   176\t\t\t\t\tmsg = o.str();\n   177\t\t\t\t\treturn false;\n   178\t\t\t\t}\n   179\t\t\treturn true;\n   180\t\t}\n   181\t};\n   182\t\n   183\t#endif\n\n     1\t// Set partitioning branch-and-price. Only continuous variables reach Gurobi.\n     2\t#include \"branchprice.h\"\n     3\t#include \"binpacking.h\"\n     4\t#include \"gurobi_c++.h\"\n     5\t#include <algorithm>\n     6\t#include <cmath>\n     7\t#include <cstddef>\n     8\t#include <functional>\n     9\t#include <iostream>\n    10\t#include <map>\n    11\t#include <numeric>\n    12\t#include <queue>\n    13\t#include <random>\n    14\t#include <set>\n    15\t#include <stdexcept>\n    16\t\n    17\tnamespace\n    18\t{\n    19\tusing Clock = std::chrono::steady_clock;\n    20\tusing Pattern = std::vector<int>;\n    21\tconstexpr double RC_EPS = 1e-7;\n    22\tconstexpr double INT_EPS = 1e-6;\n    23\tstruct TimeExpired\n    24\t{\n    25\t};\n    26\tvoid checkTime(Clock::time_point deadline)\n    27\t{\n    28\t\tif (Clock::now() >= deadline)\n    29\t\t\tthrow TimeExpired{};\n    30\t}\n    31\t\n    32\tstruct Node\n    33\t{\n    34\t\tstd::vector<std::pair<int, int>> together, apart;\n    35\t\tint bound;\n    36\t\tint depth = 0;\n    37\t\tunsigned long long id;\n    38\t};\n    39\tstruct BestBound\n    40\t{\n    41\t\tbool operator()(const Node &a, const Node &b) const\n    42\t\t{\n    43\t\t\treturn a.bound != b.bound   ? a.bound > b.bound\n    44\t\t\t       : a.depth != b.depth ? a.depth < b.depth\n    45\t\t\t                            : a.id > b.id;\n    46\t\t}\n    47\t};\n    48\tstruct Groups\n    49\t{\n    50\t\tstd::vector<Pattern> items;\n    51\t\tstd::vector<long long> size;\n    52\t\tstd::vector<std::vector<bool>> conflict;\n    53\t\tstd::vector<int> group_of;\n    54\t\tbool valid = true;\n    55\t\tGroups(const BinPackingData &d, const Node &node, Clock::time_point deadline)\n    56\t\t{\n    57\t\t\tstd::vector<int> parent(d.n + 1);\n    58\t\t\tstd::iota(parent.begin(), parent.end(), 0);\n    59\t\t\tstd::function<int(int)> root = [&](int i)\n    60\t\t\t{ return parent[i] == i ? i : parent[i] = root(parent[i]); };\n    61\t\t\tfor (auto [i, j] : node.together)\n    62\t\t\t\tparent[root(i)] = root(j);\n    63\t\t\tstd::map<int, int> ids;\n    64\t\t\tgroup_of.resize(d.n + 1);\n    65\t\t\tfor (int i = 1; i <= d.n; ++i)\n    66\t\t\t{\n    67\t\t\t\tcheckTime(deadline);\n    68\t\t\t\tint r = root(i);\n    69\t\t\t\tauto it = ids.emplace(r, static_cast<int>(ids.size())).first;\n    70\t\t\t\tint g = it->second;\n    71\t\t\t\tif (g == static_cast<int>(items.size()))\n    72\t\t\t\t{\n    73\t\t\t\t\titems.emplace_back();\n    74\t\t\t\t\tsize.push_back(0);\n    75\t\t\t\t}\n    76\t\t\t\tgroup_of[i] = g;\n    77\t\t\t\titems[g].push_back(i);\n    78\t\t\t\tif (d.size[i] > d.C - size[g])\n    79\t\t\t\t{\n    80\t\t\t\t\tvalid = false;\n    81\t\t\t\t\treturn;\n    82\t\t\t\t}\n    83\t\t\t\tsize[g] += d.size[i];\n    84\t\t\t}\n    85\t\t\tconflict.assign(items.size(), std::vector<bool>(items.size(), false));\n    86\t\t\tauto forbid = [&](int i, int j)\n    87\t\t\t{\n    88\t\t\t\tcheckTime(deadline);\n    89\t\t\t\tint a = group_of[i], b = group_of[j];\n    90\t\t\t\tif (a == b)\n    91\t\t\t\t\tvalid = false;\n    92\t\t\t\telse\n    93\t\t\t\t\tconflict[a][b] = conflict[b][a] = true;\n    94\t\t\t};\n    95\t\t\tfor (const auto &s : d.cls)\n    96\t\t\t\tfor (size_t i = 0; i < s.size(); ++i)\n    97\t\t\t\t\tfor (size_t j = i + 1; j < s.size(); ++j)\n    98\t\t\t\t\t\tforbid(s[i], s[j]);\n    99\t\t\tfor (auto [i, j] : node.apart)\n   100\t\t\t\tforbid(i, j);\n   101\t\t}\n   102\t\tPattern expand(const Pattern &p) const\n   103\t\t{\n   104\t\t\tPattern result;\n   105\t\t\tfor (int g : p)\n   106\t\t\t\tresult.insert(result.end(), items[g].begin(), items[g].end());\n   107\t\t\tstd::sort(result.begin(), result.end());\n   108\t\t\treturn result;\n   109\t\t}\n   110\t\tbool admissible(const Pattern &p, Pattern &out) const\n   111\t\t{\n   112\t\t\tstd::vector<int> counts(items.size(), 0);\n   113\t\t\tfor (int i : p)\n   114\t\t\t\t++counts[group_of[i]];\n   115\t\t\tout.clear();\n   116\t\t\tfor (size_t g = 0; g < counts.size(); ++g)\n   117\t\t\t{\n   118\t\t\t\tif (!counts[g])\n   119\t\t\t\t\tcontinue;\n   120\t\t\t\tif (counts[g] != static_cast<int>(items[g].size()))\n   121\t\t\t\t\treturn false;\n   122\t\t\t\tfor (int h : out)\n   123\t\t\t\t\tif (conflict[g][h])\n   124\t\t\t\t\t\treturn false;\n   125\t\t\t\tout.push_back(static_cast<int>(g));\n   126\t\t\t}\n   127\t\t\treturn !out.empty();\n   128\t\t}\n   129\t};\n   130\t\n   131\tclass DeadlineCallback : public GRBCallback\n   132\t{\n   133\t\tClock::time_point deadline;\n   134\t\tvoid callback() override\n   135\t\t{\n   136\t\t\tif (Clock::now() >= deadline)\n   137\t\t\t\tabort();\n   138\t\t}\n   139\t\n   140\t  public:\n   141\t\texplicit DeadlineCallback(Clock::time_point d) : deadline(d) {}\n   142\t};\n   143\t\n   144\t// Fractional knapsack, ignoring conflicts, upper-bounds all feasible pricing\n   145\t// patterns. Positive duals only: removing a nonpositive group preserves validity.\n   146\tstruct Pricing\n   147\t{\n   148\t\tconst Groups &g;\n   149\t\tconst std::vector<double> &u;\n   150\t\tlong long capacity;\n   151\t\tClock::time_point deadline;\n   152\t\tPattern order, chosen, best_pattern;\n   153\t\tlong double best = 0, upper = 0;\n   154\t\tbool complete = true;\n   155\t\tPricing(const Groups &groups, const std::vector<double> &duals, long long c,\n   156\t\t        Clock::time_point d)\n   157\t\t    : g(groups), u(duals), capacity(c), deadline(d)\n   158\t\t{\n   159\t\t\tfor (size_t i = 0; i < u.size(); ++i)\n   160\t\t\t\tif (u[i] > 0)\n   161\t\t\t\t\torder.push_back(static_cast<int>(i));\n   162\t\t\tstd::sort(order.begin(), order.end(),\n   163\t\t\t          [&](int a, int b)\n   164\t\t\t          {\n   165\t\t\t\t          long double x = static_cast<long double>(u[a]) * g.size[b];\n   166\t\t\t\t          long double y = static_cast<long double>(u[b]) * g.size[a];\n   167\t\t\t\t          return x != y ? x > y : a < b;\n   168\t\t\t          });\n   169\t\t\tupper = fractional(0, capacity);\n   170\t\t}\n   171\t\tlong double fractional(size_t pos, long long remaining) const\n   172\t\t{\n   173\t\t\tlong double value = 0;\n   174\t\t\tfor (size_t p = pos; p < order.size() && remaining > 0; ++p)\n   175\t\t\t{\n   176\t\t\t\tint i = order[p];\n   177\t\t\t\tif (g.size[i] <= remaining)\n   178\t\t\t\t{\n   179\t\t\t\t\tremaining -= g.size[i];\n   180\t\t\t\t\tvalue += u[i];\n   181\t\t\t\t}\n   182\t\t\t\telse\n   183\t\t\t\t{\n   184\t\t\t\t\tvalue += static_cast<long double>(u[i]) * remaining / g.size[i];\n   185\t\t\t\t\tbreak;\n   186\t\t\t\t}\n   187\t\t\t}\n   188\t\t\treturn value;\n   189\t\t}\n   190\t\tvoid dfs(size_t pos, long long remaining, long double value)\n   191\t\t{\n   192\t\t\tif (Clock::now() >= deadline)\n   193\t\t\t{\n   194\t\t\t\tcomplete = false;\n   195\t\t\t\treturn;\n   196\t\t\t}\n   197\t\t\tif (value > best)\n   198\t\t\t{\n   199\t\t\t\tbest = value;\n   200\t\t\t\tbest_pattern = chosen;\n   201\t\t\t}\n   202\t\t\tif (pos == order.size() || value + fractional(pos, remaining) <= best + 1e-10L)\n   203\t\t\t\treturn;\n   204\t\t\tint i = order[pos];\n   205\t\t\tbool ok = g.size[i] <= remaining;\n   206\t\t\tfor (int j : chosen)\n   207\t\t\t\tif (g.conflict[i][j])\n   208\t\t\t\t{\n   209\t\t\t\t\tok = false;\n   210\t\t\t\t\tbreak;\n   211\t\t\t\t}\n   212\t\t\tif (ok)\n   213\t\t\t{\n   214\t\t\t\tchosen.push_back(i);\n   215\t\t\t\tdfs(pos + 1, remaining - g.size[i], value + u[i]);\n   216\t\t\t\tchosen.pop_back();\n   217\t\t\t\tif (!complete)\n   218\t\t\t\t\treturn;\n   219\t\t\t}\n   220\t\t\tdfs(pos + 1, remaining, value);\n   221\t\t}\n   222\t\tvoid exact()\n   223\t\t{\n   224\t\t\tdfs(0, capacity, 0);\n   225\t\t\t// Account for the small pruning tolerance and floating arithmetic.\n   226\t\t\tif (complete)\n   227\t\t\t\tupper = best + 1e-8L;\n   228\t\t}\n   229\t};\n   230\t\n   231\ttemplate <typename AddColumn>\n   232\tstd::size_t addHeuristicColumns(const Pricing &pricing, std::mt19937_64 &rng, const AddColumn &add)\n   233\t{\n   234\t\tconst auto &groups = pricing.g;\n   235\t\tconst auto &dual = pricing.u;\n   236\t\tstd::size_t added = 0;\n   237\t\t// Try density order first, then dual value, then shuffled orders.\n   238\t\tfor (int round = 0; round < 12 && Clock::now() < pricing.deadline; ++round)\n   239\t\t{\n   240\t\t\tPattern order = pricing.order;\n   241\t\t\tif (round == 1)\n   242\t\t\t\tstd::sort(order.begin(), order.end(),\n   243\t\t\t\t          [&](int a, int b) { return dual[a] != dual[b] ? dual[a] > dual[b] : a < b; });\n   244\t\t\telse if (round > 1)\n   245\t\t\t\tstd::shuffle(order.begin(), order.end(), rng);\n   246\t\t\tPattern p;\n   247\t\t\tlong long remaining = pricing.capacity;\n   248\t\t\tlong double value = 0;\n   249\t\t\tfor (int g : order)\n   250\t\t\t{\n   251\t\t\t\tif (groups.size[g] > remaining)\n   252\t\t\t\t\tcontinue;\n   253\t\t\t\tbool ok = true;\n   254\t\t\t\tfor (int h : p)\n   255\t\t\t\t\tif (groups.conflict[g][h])\n   256\t\t\t\t\t{\n   257\t\t\t\t\t\tok = false;\n   258\t\t\t\t\t\tbreak;\n   259\t\t\t\t\t}\n   260\t\t\t\tif (ok)\n   261\t\t\t\t{\n   262\t\t\t\t\tp.push_back(g);\n   263\t\t\t\t\tremaining -= groups.size[g];\n   264\t\t\t\t\tvalue += dual[g];\n   265\t\t\t\t}\n   266\t\t\t}\n   267\t\t\tif (value > 1 + RC_EPS && add(p))\n   268\t\t\t\t++added;\n   269\t\t}\n   270\t\treturn added;\n   271\t}\n   272\t\n   273\tstatic void roundingHeuristics(BinPackingData &data, const std::set<Pattern> &pool, GRBEnv &env,\n\n   274\t\tClock::time_point deadline, double max_seconds) \n   275\t{\n   276\t\t\tdouble left = std::chrono::duration<double>(deadline - Clock::now()).count();\n   277\t\t\tif (left <= 0) { \n   278\t\t\t\treturn; \n   279\t\t\t}\n   280\t\n   281\t        GRBModel mip(env);\n   282\t        mip.set(GRB_DoubleParam_TimeLimit, std::min(max_seconds, left));\n   283\t        mip.set(GRB_DoubleParam_Cutoff, data.best_value - 0.5);\n   284\t\n   285\t\t\tstd::vector<GRBConstr> cover(data.n + 1);\n   286\t        for (int i = 1; i <= data.n; ++i) {\n   287\t\t\t\tcover[i] = mip.addConstr(GRBLinExpr(0.0) >= 1.0);\n   288\t\t\t}\n   289\t\n   290\t\t\tstd::vector<const Pattern *> cols;\n   291\t        std::vector<GRBVar> y;\n   292\t        for (const auto &p : pool)\n   293\t        {\n   294\t                GRBColumn c;\n   295\t                for (int i : p)\n   296\t                        c.addTerm(1.0, cover[i]);\n   297\t                y.push_back(mip.addVar(0.0, 1.0, 1.0, GRB_BINARY, c));\n   298\t                cols.push_back(&p);\n   299\t        }\n   300\t\n   301\t        mip.optimize();\n   302\t        if (mip.get(GRB_IntAttr_SolCount) == 0) {\n   303\t\t\t\treturn; \n   304\t\t\t}\n   305\t\n   306\t\t\tstd::vector<bool> placed(data.n + 1, false);\n   307\t        std::vector<Pattern> bins;\n   308\t        for (size_t k = 0; k < y.size(); ++k)\n   309\t                if (y[k].get(GRB_DoubleAttr_X) > 0.5)\n   310\t                {\n   311\t                        Pattern b;\n   312\t                        for (int i : *cols[k])\n   313\t                                if (!placed[i])\n   314\t                                        placed[i] = true, b.push_back(i);\n   315\t                        if (!b.empty())\n   316\t                                bins.push_back(b);\n   317\t                }\n   318\t\n   319\t\t\tstd::string error;\n   320\t        if (data.CheckSolution(bins, error) && (int)bins.size() < data.best_value) {\n   321\t\t\t\tdata.UpdateBest(bins);\n   322\t\t\t}\n   323\t\n   324\t}\n   325\t \n   326\tint certifiedBound(const std::vector<double> &u, long double alpha, int inherited)\n   327\t{\n   328\t\tlong double sum = 0;\n   329\t\tfor (double x : u)\n   330\t\t\tsum += x;\n   331\t\tlong double safe_alpha = std::max(1.0L, alpha) + 1e-8L;\n   332\t\treturn std::max(inherited, static_cast<int>(std::ceil(sum / safe_alpha - INT_EPS)));\n   333\t}\n   334\t\n   335\tstruct Stats\n   336\t{\n   337\t\tsize_t nodes = 0, lp = 0, heuristic = 0, exact = 0, branches = 0;\n   338\t};\n   339\t} // namespace\n   340\t\n   341\tvoid BranchAndPrice(BinPackingData &data, Clock::time_point deadline)\n   342\t{\n   343\t\tstd::cout << \"Starting Branch-and-Price\\n\";\n   344\t\tstd::priority_queue<Node, std::vector<Node>, BestBound> open;\n   345\t\tunsigned long long next_id = 1;\n   346\t\topen.push(Node{{}, {}, data.lower_bound, 0, 0}\n   347\t\t);\n   348\t\tstd::set<Pattern> pool;\n   349\t\tfor (int i = 1; i <= data.n; ++i)\n   350\t\t\tpool.insert({i});\n   351\t\n   352\t\tfor (auto bin : data.best_solution)\n   353\t\t{\n   354\t\t\tstd::sort(bin.begin(), bin.end());\n   355\t\t\tpool.insert(bin);\n   356\t\t}\n   357\t\n   358\t\tStats stats;\n   359\t\tstd::mt19937_64 rng(data.seed);\n   360\t\n   361\t\ttry\n   362\t\t{\n   363\t\t\tGRBEnv env(true);\n   364\t\t\t// env.set(GRB_IntParam_OutputFlag, 0);\n   365\t\t\tenv.set(GRB_IntParam_OutputFlag, 1);\n   366\t\t\tenv.set(GRB_IntParam_Threads, 1);\n   367\t\t\tenv.start();\n   368\t\t\tDeadlineCallback callback(deadline);\n   369\t\t\twhile (!open.empty() && Clock::now() < deadline)\n   370\t\t\t{\n   371\t\t\t\tif (open.top().bound >= current node in the frontier until it has been resolved or\n   379\t\t\t\t// replaced by BOTH children. Thus timeouts cannot lose a subtree bound.\n   380\t\t\t\topen.push(node);\n   381\t\t\t\t++stats.nodes;\n   382\t\t\t\tGroups groups(data, node, deadline);\n   383\t\t\t\tif (!groups.valid)\n   384\t\t\t\t{\n   385\t\t\t\t\topen.pop();\n   386\t\t\t\t\tcontinue;\n   387\t\t\t\t}\n   388\t\t\t\tGRBModel model(env);\n   389\t\t\t\tmodel.setCallback(&callback);\n   390\t\t\t\tmodel.set(GRB_IntParam_Method, 1); g < groups.items.size(); ++g)\n   414\t\t\t\t{\n   415\t\t\t\t\tcheckTime(deadline);\n   416\t\t\t\t\tadd({static_cast<int>(g)});\n   417\t\t\t\t}\n   418\t\t\t\t// Snapshot: adding a column also inserts its expansion into the pool.\n   419\t\t\t\tstd::vector<Pattern> snapshot(pool.begin(), pool.end());\n   420\t\t\t\tfor (const auto &p : snapshot)\n   421\t\t\t\t{\n   422\t\t\t\t\tcheckTime(deadline);\n   423\t\t\t\t\tPattern q;\n   424\t\t\t\t\tif (groups.admissible(p, q))\n   425\t\t\t\t\t\tadd(q);\n   426\t\t\t\t}\n   427\t\t\t\tbool solved = false, stopped = false, branched = false;\n   428\t\t\t\twhile (Clock::now() < deadline)\n   429\t\t\t\t{\n   430\t\t\t\t\tmodel.set(\n   431\t\t\t\t\t    GRB_DoubleParam_TimeLimit,\n   432\t\t\t\t\t    std::max(0.0, std::chrono::duration<double>(deadline - Clock::now()).count()));\n   433\t\t\t\t\tmodel.optimize();\n   434\t\t\t\t\t++stats.lp;\n   435\t\t\t\t\t// Por que != de GRB_OPTIMAL\n   436\t\t\t\t\tif (model.get(GRB_IntAttr_Status) != GRB_OPTIMAL)\n   437\t\t\t\t\t{\n   438\t\t\t\t\t\tstopped = true;\n   439\t\t\t\t\t\tbreak;\n   440\t\t\t\t\t}\n   441\t\t\t\t\tstd::vector<double> dual(rows.size());\n   442\t\t\t\t\tfor (size_t g = 0; p < vars.size(); ++p)\n   492\t\t\t\t\t{\n   493\t\t\t\t\t\tx[p] = vars[p].get(GRB_DoubleAttr_X);\n   494\t\t\t\t\t\tif (std::abs(x[p] - std::round(x[p])) > INT_EPS)\n   495\t\t\t\t\t\t\tintegral = false;\n   496\t\t\t\t\t}\n   497\t\n   498\t\t\t\t\t// Rounding Heuristics\n   499\t\t\t\t\tif (node.depth == 0 || stats.nodes % 50 == 0) {\n   500\t\t\t\t\t\troundingHeuristics(data, pool, env, deadline, 5.0);\n   501\t\t\t\t\t\tif (node.bound >= data.best_value) {\n   502\t\t\t\t\t\t\tsolved = true;\n   503\t\t\t\t\t\t\tbreak;\n   504\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n   505\t\t\t\t\t}\n   506\t\n   507\t\t\t\t\tif (integral)\n   508\t\t\t\t\t{\n   509\t\t\t\t\t\tstd::vector<Pattern> bins;\n   510\t\t\t\t\t\tfor (size_t p = 0; p < x.size(); ++p)\n   511\t\t\t\t\t\t\tif (x[p] > 0.5)\n   512\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\tbins.push_back(groups.expand(patterns[p]));\n   513\t\t\t\t\t\tstd::string error;\n   514\t\t\t\t\t\tif (!data.CheckSolution(bins, error))\n   515\t\t\t\t\t\t\tthrow std::runtime_error(error);\n   516\t\t\t\t\t\tdata.UpdateBest(bins);\n   517\t\t\t\t\t\tsolved = true;\n   518\t\t\t\t\t\tbreak;\n   519\t\t\t\t\t}\n   520\t\t\t\t\tstd::map<std::pair<int, int>, double> phi;\n   521\t\t\t\t\tfor (size_t p = 0; // parent already replaced by both children\n   562\t\t\t\topen.pop();\n   563\t\t\t\tif (!solved)\n   564\t\t\t\t{\n   565\t\t\t\t\topen.push(node);\n   566\t\t\t\t\tstopped = true;\n   567\t\t\t\t}\n   568\t\t\t\tif (stopped)\n   569\t\t\t\t\tbreak;\n   570\t\t\t}\n   571\t\t}\n   572\t\tcatch (const TimeExpired &)\n   573\t\t{\n   574\t\t\t// The current node is still in the frontier with its inherited bound.\n   575\t\t}\n   576\t\tcatch (const GRBException &e)\n   577\t\t{\n   578\t\t\tstd::cerr << \"Gurobi: \" << e.getMessage() << \" (\" << e.getErrorCode()\n   579\t\t\t          << \"). Returning incumbent and certified frontier bound.\\n\";\n   580\t\t}\n   581\t\tcatch (const std::exception &e)\n   582\t\t{\n   583\t\t\tstd::cerr << \"Search interrupted: \" << e.what() << \". AGENTS.md aponta para docs/CONTEXTO_CODEX.md, que\\n   254\\tcontém o estado do trabalho e um prompt para retomar a colaboração.\\n   255\\tO pacote contém os arquivos atuais, inclusive alterações não commitadas;\\n   256\\tnão contém .git, executáveis, dependências instaladas ou licença Gurobi.\\n   257\\tÉ um contexto portátil para um novo chat; não importa uma sessão interna.\\n   258\\tPara atualizar a conversa antes de exportar, forneça --sessao ao script.\\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"Package only submission sources/text; optionally export this Codex session.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport re\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\nimport time\\nimport zipfile\\n\\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent\\nSOURCE = 'src' if (ROOT / 'src').is_dir() else 'codigo'\\nAI = 'ai' if (ROOT / 'ai').is_dir() else 'ia'\\nALLOWED = {'.cpp', '.h', '.py', '.txt', '.md', '.json', '.jsonl', '.pdf'}\\n\\n\\nREMINDER = re.compile(r'<system-reminder>.*?</system-reminder>\\\\s*', re.S)\\n\\n\\ndef _clean(text):\\n    # Internal application instructions are not part of the conversation.\\n    return REMINDER.sub('', text).strip()\\n\\n\\ndef _tool_output(content):\\n    if isinstance(content, str):\\n        return _clean(content)\\n    parts = []\\n    for part in content or []:\\n        if isinstance(part, dict) and part.get('type') == 'text':\\n            parts.append(_clean(part['text']))\\n        elif isinstance(part, dict):\\n            parts.append(f\\\"[{part.get('type', 'conteúdo')} omitido]\\\")\\n    return '\\\\n'.join(parts)\\n\\n\\ndef read_claude_records(session):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Claude Code transcript: keeps prompts, answers and tool calls/results;\\n    drops thinking, attachments, system entries and request metadata.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    records = []\\n    complete = False\\n    for line in session.read_text().splitlines():\\n        try:\\n            item = json.loads(line)\\n        except json.JSONDecodeError:\\n            continue\\n        kind = item.get('type')\\n        if kind not in ('user', 'assistant') or item.get('isMeta') or item.get('isSidechain'):\\n            continue\\n        message = item.get('message', {})\\n        content = message.get('content')\\n        stamp = item.get('timestamp')\\n        if isinstance(content, str):\\n            content = [dict(type='text', text=content)]\\n        for part in content or []:\\n            if not isinstance(part, dict):\\n                continue\\n            ptype = part.get('type')\\n            if ptype == 'text':\\n                text = _clean(part['text'])\\n                if text:\\n                    records.append(dict(timestamp=stamp, type='message', role=kind,\\n                                        content=[dict(type='text', text=text)]))\\n            elif ptype == 'tool_use':\\n                records.append(dict(timestamp=stamp, type='function_call', call_id=part.get('id'),\\n                                    name=part.get('name'),\\n                                    arguments=json.dumps(part.get('input', {}), ensure_ascii=False)))\\n            elif ptype == 'tool_result':\\n                records.append(dict(timestamp=stamp, type='function_call_output',\\n                                    call_id=part.get('tool_use_id'),\\n                                    output=_tool_output(part.get('content'))))\\n        if kind == 'assistant':\\n            complete = message.get('stop_reason') == 'end_turn'\\n        elif any(isinstance(p, dict) and p.get('type') in ('text', 'tool_result')\\n                 for p in content or []):\\n            complete = False\\n    return records, complete\\n\\n\\ndef is_claude_session(session):\\n    for line in session.read_text().splitlines():\\n        try:\\n            item = json.loads(line)\\n        except json.JSONDecodeError:\\n            continue\\n        if item.get('type') in ('response_item', 'event_msg', 'session_meta'):\\n            return False\\n        if item.get('type') in ('user', 'assistant') and 'message' in item:\\n            return True\\n    return False\\n\\n\\ndef read_records(session):\\n    if is_claude_session(session):\\n        return read_claude_records(session)\\n    records = []\\n    complete = False\\n    for line in session.read_text().splitlines():\\n        try:\\n            item = json.loads(line)\\n        except json.JSONDecodeError:\\n            continue  # a writer may still be appending the last line\\n        payload = item.get('payload', {})\\n        kind = item.get('type')\\n        if kind == 'event_msg':\\n            if payload.get('type') == 'task_started':\\n                complete = False\\n            elif payload.get('type') == 'task_complete':\\n                complete = True\\n        if kind != 'response_item':\\n            continue\\n        kind = payload.get('type')\\n        if kind == 'message' and payload.get('role') in ('user', 'assistant'):\\n            records.append(dict(timestamp=item.get('timestamp'), type=kind,\\n                                role=payload['role'], content=payload.get('content', [])))\\n        elif kind in ('custom_tool_call', 'custom_tool_call_output',\\n                      'function_call', 'function_call_output'):\\n            records.append(dict(timestamp=item.get('timestamp'), **{\\n                key: value for key, value in payload.items()\\n                if key in ('type', 'call_id', 'name', 'input', 'arguments', 'output')}))\\n    return records, complete\\n\\n\\ndef export(session, suffix=''):\\n    records, complete = read_records(session)\\n    if not records:\\n        raise ValueError(f'Session has no conversation records: {session}')\\n    date = records[0]['timestamp'][:10] + (f'-{suffix}' if suffix else '')\\n    folder = ROOT / AI\\n    folder.mkdir(exist_ok=True)\\n    (folder / f'conversa-{date}.jsonl').write_text(''.join(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + '\\\\n'\\n                                                        for record in records))\\n    sections = ['# Conversa com IA\\\\n\\\\nRegistro cronológico dos prompts, respostas e ferramentas.\\\\n']\\n    for record in records:\\n        label = record.get('role', record['type'])\\n        sections.append(f\\\"\\\\n## {record.get('timestamp')} — {label}\\\\n\\\")\\n        if record['type'] == 'message':\\n            for part in record['content']:\\n                if isinstance(part, dict) and 'text' in part:\\n                    sections.append(part['text'] + '\\\\n')\\n                else:\\n                    sections.append(json.dumps(part, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + '\\\\n')\\n        else:\\n            # JSON also preserves exact newlines/escaping of tool inputs/outputs.\\n            sections.append('```json\\\\n' + json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + '\\\\n```\\\\n')\\n    (folder / f'conversa-{date}.md').write_text('\\\\n'.join(sections))\\n    (folder / f'estado-{date}.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(\\n        session=session.name, records=len(records), turn_complete=complete), indent=2) + '\\\\n')\\n    return complete\\n\\n\\n\"}"

--exato compara com um ótimo independente.\"\"\"\n     3\timport argparse\n     4\tfrom pathlib import Path\n     5\timport sys\n     6\tfrom verificar import read_instance, oracle, check_output\n     7\t\n     8\t\n     9\tdef main():\n    10\t    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n    11\t    parser.add_argument('-f', '--entrada', type=Path, required=True)\n    12\t    parser.add_argument('-o', '--saida', type=Path, required=True)\n    13\t    parser.add_argument('--exato', action='store_true', help='Calcula OPT por DP, até 16 itens')\n    14\t    args = parser.parse_args()\n    15\t    if not __debug__:\n    16\t        parser.error('Execute sem -O/PYTHONOPTIMIZE: as verificações usam assert.')\n    17\t    try:\n    18\t        sizes, capacity, classes = read_instance(args.entrada)\n    19\t        lines = [line.strip() for line in args.saida.read_text().splitlines()\n    20\t                 if line.strip() and not line.lstrip().startswith('#')]\n    21\t        ub, lb = map(int, lines[:2])\n    22\t        if args.exato and len(sizes) > 16:\n    23\t            parser.error('--exato aceita no máximo 16 itens (custo exponencial).')\n    24\t        optimum = oracle(sizes, capacity, classes) if args.exato else ub\n    25\t        check_output(args.saida, sizes, capacity, classes, optimum)\n    26\t    except (AssertionError, ValueError, IndexError, StopIteration, OSError) as error:\n    27\t        print(f'FALHA: saída inconsistente/inviável ou limitante incompatível. {error}', file=sys.stderr)\n    28\t        return 1\n    29\t    print(f'VIÁVEL: {ub} recipientes; partição, capacidade e conflitos conferidos.')\n    30\t    if args.exato:\n    31\t        print(f'Ótimo independente: {optimum}; limitante informado: {lb}.')\n    32\t        print('SOLUÇÃO ÓTIMA confirmada pelo oráculo.' if ub == optimum\n    33\t              else f'Solução ainda usa {ub-optimum} recipiente(s) além do ótimo.')\n    34\t    else:\n    35\t        print('O limitante inferior e a otimalidade não foram certificados por este comando.')\n    36\t    return 0\n    37\t\n    38\t\n    39\tif __name__ == '__main__':\n    40\t    sys.exit(main())\n# Forward commands to the submission's source directory.\n.PHONY: all run test clean lint format format-check\nall run test clean lint format format-check:\n\t$(MAKE) -C src $@\n\t// True if the items i and j (i != j) cannot be in the same bin.\n\tbool Conflict(int i, int j) const\n\t{\n\t\tif (i == j)\n\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\tconst std::vector<int> &a = classes_of[i], &b = classes_of[j];\n\t\tsize_t p = 0, q = 0; // both lists are sorted (see Prepare)\n\t\twhile (p < a.size() && q < b.size())\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tif (a[p] == b[q])\n\t\t\t\treturn true;\n\t\t\tif (a[p] < b[q])\n\t\t\t\tp++;\n\t\t\telse\n\t\t\t\tq++;\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn false;\n\t}\n\n\t// A simple valid lower bound: the total size divided by C (rounded up), and\n\t// the size of the largest class (its items must be in different bins).\n\tint TrivialLowerBound() const\n\t{\n\t\t// Incremental quotient/remainder avoids overflow even when sum(size)\n\t\t// exceeds LLONG_MAX. Since size[i] <= C, the quotient never exceeds n.\n\t\tint lb = 0;\n\t\tlong long remainder = 0;\n\t\tfor (int i = 1; i <= n; i++)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tif (size[i] >= C - remainder)\n\t\t\t{\n\t\t\t\t++lb;\n\t\t\t\tremainder = size[i] - (C - remainder);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\telse\n\t\t\t\tremainder += size[i];\n\t\t}\n\t\tif (remainder)\n\t\t\t++lb;\n\t\tfor (int t = 0; t < k; t++)\n\t\t\tlb = std::max(lb, (int)cls[t].size());\n\t\treturn lb;\n\t}\n\n\t// Updates the best solution if the given packing is better. Returns true\n\t// if it was stored.\n\tbool UpdateBest(const std::vector<std::vector<int>> &bins)\n\t{\n\t\tif ((int)bins.size() >= best_value)\n\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\tbest_solution = bins;\n\t\tbest_value = (int)bins.size();\n\t\treturn true;\n\t}\n\n\t// Checks whether `bins` is a feasible packing: every item appears in exactly\n\t// one bin, no bin exceeds C, and no bin contains two items of a same class.\n\t// On failure, a message describing the first violation is stored in `msg`.\n\tbool CheckSolution(const std::vector<std::vector<int>> &bins, std::string &msg) const\n\t{\n\t\tstd::vector<int> seen(n + 1, 0);\n\t\tfor (size_t b = 0; b < bins.size(); b++)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tlong long load = 0;\n\t\t\tfor (size_t a = 0; a < bins[b].size(); a++)\n\t\t\t{\n\t\t\t\tint i = bins[b][a];\n\t\t\t\tif (i < 1 || i > n)\n\t\t\t\t{\n\t\t\t\t\tstd::ostringstream o;\n\t\t\t\t\to << \"bin \" << b + 1 << \" contains invalid item \" << i;\n\t\t\t\t\tmsg = o.str();\n\t\t\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif (++seen[i] > 1)\n\t\t\t\t{\n\t\t\t\t\tstd::ostringstream o;\n\t\t\t\t\to << \"item \" << i << \" appears more than once\";\n\t\t\t\t\tmsg = o.str();\n\t\t\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\t\t\t}\n"
    }

**Ainda faltam ajustes antes de enviar.** O núcleo do branch-and-price passou na validação independente. Os problemas de entrega estão nos comandos reproduzíveis, no limite de tempo, na gravação da saída e na exportação das conversas de IA.

### O que falta corrigir

1. **`make test` falha no pacote extraído.** A suíte passa todas as instâncias ao oráculo exponencial de subconjuntos, incluindo casos de 60 a 360 itens. Reproduzi um `OverflowError`. Mantenha a comparação com o ótimo para os casos pequenos e verifique os maiores em uma etapa própria. [verificar.py](/home/tuca/Documents/bnp-bin-packing/testes/verificar.py:95)
2. **`make run` falha com os parâmetros padrão.** O programa resolveu a instância, mas o destino padrão fica em `tests-results/`, pasta ausente no ZIP. A compilação funcionou com Gurobi 13.0.3 configurado. Em um Linux sem `GUROBI_HOME` no ambiente, o `make` tentou usar o caminho padrão do macOS e falhou. Ajuste o destino e documente o comando de compilação a partir de `codigo/`. [Makefile](/home/tuca/Documents/bnp-bin-packing/src/Makefile:4)
3. **O prazo máximo do enunciado ainda não é cumprido literalmente.** O cronômetro começa depois da leitura. Com uma entrada válida e `-t 0.005`, medi **15 ms** de execução, enquanto o solver registrou `0.000 s`. O MIP opcional também calcula o tempo restante antes de montar o modelo. A leitura e a montagem precisam entrar na revisão do orçamento. [main.cpp](/home/tuca/Documents/bnp-bin-packing/src/main.cpp:230) · [binpacksolver.cpp](/home/tuca/Documents/bnp-bin-packing/src/binpacksolver.cpp:145) · [branchprice.cpp](/home/tuca/Documents/bnp-bin-packing/src/branchprice.cpp:273)
4. **Uma falha de gravação pode passar como sucesso.** Com `-o /dev/full`, o executável retornou código `0`. O gravador precisa conferir o estado do arquivo após finalizar a escrita. [main.cpp](/home/tuca/Documents/bnp-bin-packing/src/main.cpp:151)
5. **As conversas de IA estão incompletas.** A sessão Claude `9980d17d` tem 117 registros exportados de 139 disponíveis. A auditoria Claude `5f9bf747` também está fora da pasta `ai/`. Atualize essas exportações e inclua esta conversa ao final, conforme a seção 11. [estado da exportação](/home/tuca/Documents/bnp-bin-packing/ai/estado-2026-11-20-claude-9980d17d.json) · [enunciado](/home/tuca/Documents/bnp-bin-packing/enunciado.pdf)

O **README também precisa refletir o código atual**. Ele diz que não há MIP, mas a heurística opcional já usa variáveis binárias. O mestre do branch-and-price continua linear. `make format-check` falhou em `branchprice.cpp`, e o lint terminou com seis avisos. [README](/home/tuca/Documents/bnp-bin-packing/README:78) · [branchprice.cpp](/home/tuca/Documents/bnp-bin-packing/src/branchprice.cpp:273)

### O que passou

Compilei a partir de um ZIP temporário com a estrutura exigida e sem binários. A suíte isolada confirmou **567 ótimos independentes**, incluindo o exemplo oficial (`LB=UB=4`) e o caso Ryan–Foster (`LB=UB=6`, três nós processados). As seis instâncias maiores produziram soluções viáveis com `T=1 s`. A otimalidade dessas seis não foi verificada por um oráculo independente.

A revisão preservou os fontes locais e a remoção do ZIP no Git. Depois das correções, gere e valide o ZIP final extraído. O enunciado pede **um único anexo** para `fkm@unicamp.br`, com o assunto exato **`[PLI] Bin Packing com Classes de Conflito`**. [enunciado](/home/tuca/Documents/bnp-bin-packing/enunciado.pdf)

Não reverta essas escolhas por confundi-las com\\na estrutura de entrega do enunciado.\\n\\n## Requisitos e decisões do algoritmo\\n\\n- C++17. Um padrão/coluna é um recipiente válido não vazio.\\n- Formulação de partição: minimizar soma de `x_B`, com soma das colunas que\\n  cobrem cada item igual a 1. O mestre usa variáveis contínuas na relaxação.\\n- Gurobi é usado somente para os PLs do mestre; não executa a árvore inteira.\\n- A heurística First Fit probabilística do código-base foi mantida para\\n  incumbente e colunas iniciais; unitários garantem uma saída até com T=0.\\n- A geração tenta pricing guloso primeiro (densidade, valor e ordens aleatórias).\\n  Somente quando não adiciona colunas, chama pricing exato por enumeração\\n  incluir/excluir, com poda pela mochila fracionária sem conflitos.\\n- Duais não positivos podem ser descartados por grupo no pricing.\\n- Decisões Ryan–Foster são representadas por pares juntos/separados. Union-find\\n  contrai os juntos. Capacidade, conflitos e restrições de separados são\\n  propagados aos grupos. Colunas globais são filtradas antes de reutilização.\\n- Banco global e conjunto local evitam duplicar padrões.\\n- Usa cotas de Farley com limite superior do pricing. O objetivo do mestre\\n  restrito sozinho não é usado como LB do nó.\\n- Prioriza o menor limitante; filhos herdam o limite do pai. O nó atual fica\\n  representado na fronteira até ser resolvido ou substituído por ambos filhos.\\n  Assim, uma interrupção não perde uma subárvore na cota global.\\n- A incumbente é verificada antes de aceitar uma solução inteira; `main`\\n  verifica novamente antes de gravar a saída.\\n\\nFunções: `SolveBinPacking` em `binpacksolver.cpp`; `Node`, `Groups`, `Pricing`,\\n`certifiedBound` e `BranchAndPrice` em `branchprice.cpp`; dados e verificador\\nem `binpacking.h`; parsing e validação da CLI em `commandline.h/.cpp`;\\nleitura/gravação e coordenação em `main.cpp`.\\n\\nAtualização de 19/11/2026: o parsing antes embutido em `main` foi extraído\\npara `ParseCommandLine`, com retorno `std::optional<CommandLineOptions>`.\\nOpções, validações, mensagens e código de saída foram preservados; o Makefile,\\nREADME e mapa de estudo foram atualizados. `make format-check` passou e\\n`make test GUROBI_HOME=/opt/gurobi1303/linux64` passou nas 187 instâncias,\\nincluindo os checks da CLI. Os testes exigiram execução fora do sandbox para\\na licença reconhecer o HostID. `make lint` com esse Gurobi terminou sem erros,\\ncom cinco avisos nos arquivos do solver e nenhum no módulo extraído.\\n\\n## Pedidos atendidos ao longo do chat\\n\\n1. Exportação de contexto, instruções de continuidade e pacote portátil.\\n\\n## Comandos disponíveis na raiz\\n\\n```bash\\nmake\\nmake run\\nmake run INPUT=../testes/instancias/ryan_foster.txt OUTPUT=../saida-rf.txt TIME=30 SEED=42\\nmake test\\nmake lint\\nmake format\\nmake format-check\\npython3 testes/conferir_solucao.py -f testes/instancias/exemplo.txt -o saida.txt --exato\\npython3 testes/verificar.py --random 500 --report /tmp/bnp-validacao.json\\npython3 testes/empacotar.py\\npython3 testes/exportar_contexto.py\\n```\\n\\n`INPUT` e `OUTPUT` são interpretados a partir de `src/`, pois o Makefile da\\nraiz encaminha os alvos. No ZIP acadêmico extraído, use `make -C codigo ...`.\\n\\n## Evidências já obtidas\\n\\n- Exemplo do enunciado: UB=LB=4; confirmado também por argumento manual e DP.\\n- Caso `ryan_foster.txt`: junção de dois ciclos C5, LP=5 e OPT=6. A execução\\n  validada processou três nós e uma ramificação, exercitando os dois filhos.\\n- Suíte padrão: 187 instâncias comparadas com ótimo por DP independente;\\n  todas com UB=LB=OPT, uma ramificação e 105 chamadas de pricing exato.\\n- Rodada ampliada: 567 instâncias, todas com valores corretos; 255 chamadas\\n  de pricing exato. O exportador\\n235-lê o registro local da sessão explicitamente indicada (veja ai/README).\\n    return complete\\n\\n\\ndef package():\\n    paths = [ROOT / 'README', ROOT / '.clang-tidy', ROOT / '.clang-format']\\n    for required in (f'{SOURCE}/Makefile', f'{SOURCE}/branchprice.cpp', f'{AI}/README'):\\n        if not (ROOT / required).is_file():\\n            raise FileNotFoundError(f'Missing required submission file: {required}')\\n    for name in (SOURCE, 'testes', AI, 'docs'):\\n        for path in sorted((ROOT / name).rglob('*')):\\n            if not path.is_file() or '__pycache__' in path.parts:\\n                continue\\n            if path.name in ('Makefile', 'README') or path.suffix in ALLOWED:\\n                paths.append(path)\\n    for path in paths:\\n        # Disallow binary files even if somebody gives one a source suffix.\\n        if path.suffix != '.pdf' and b'\\\\x00' in path.read_bytes():\\n            raise ValueError(f'Binary file in submission: {path}')\\n    output = ROOT / 'entrega-bin-packing.zip'\\n    temporary = output.with_suffix('.zip.tmp')\\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(temporary, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as archive:\\n        for path in paths:\\n            relative = path.relative_to(ROOT)\\n            # Workspace names are preserved locally; Union-find\\n  contrai os juntos. Capacidade, conflitos e restrições de separados são\\n  propagados aos grupos. name\nsnapshot_complete = empacotar.export(raw, suffix)\nexported = [json.loads(line) for line in (folder / f'conversa-{stem}.jsonl').read_text().splitlines()]\nassert exported[:len(records)] == else open('/tmp/bnp-exportacao-codex-2026-11-21-09-30.log', 'w') as log:\n        proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, '-B', '-c', launcher + worker, str(raw), turn, stem, suffix],\n
